A-083 使用基于常规全血细胞计数参数的可解释机器学习模型快速筛查急性 基孔肯雅病毒 感染。

A-083 Rapid screening for acute chikungunya virus infection using an interpretable machine learning model based on routine complete blood count parameters.
作者： Yan Liu, Dong‐De Xie, Guobiao Zhang, Yongkun Lai
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 2025年在中国佛山发生的基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)疫情凸显了快速筛查工具的迫切需求。分子诊断方法受到时间、成本和可获得性的限制。本研究旨在利用常规的全血细胞计数(CBC)参数开发并验证一种可解释的机器学习模型,以识别CHIKV感染的高风险患者。方法 本研究回顾性分析了来自佛山两家医疗机构的1,537例疑似CHIKV患者的资料。使用迈瑞BC-7500分析仪获取全血细胞计数(CBC)和细胞群体数据(CPD)。通过逆转录聚合酶链反应(RT-PCR)确认CHIKV感染。在进行严格的数据预处理和使用LASSO回归进行特征选择后,基于训练集(来自中心A的n=1,132例)开发了四个模型:逻辑回归(LR)、支持向量机(SVM)、随机森林(RF)和LightGBM(LGBM),并在内部测试集(n=192)和独立的外部测试集(来自中心B的n=213例)上进行评估。模型性能通过接收者操作特征曲线下的面积(AUC)、灵敏度、特异性和净效益进行评估。结果 在外部验证集中,LR模型表现出优越且稳定的性能(AUC = 0.807,灵敏度 = 88.0%,特异度 = 65.0%,阴性预测值 = 93.0%),显著优于基于树的模型。决策曲线分析证实了其在广泛风险阈值范围内的临床实用性。SHAP分析显示,较低的白细胞计数(WBC_N)、较低的淋巴细胞百分比(Lym%)以及增加的中性粒细胞形态异质性(D_Neu_SSC_CV)是最重要的预测因子,这与CHIKV感染的已知发病机制一致。结论 我们开发了一种基于CBC参数的可解释LR模型,能够有效筛查急性CHIKV感染,具有高灵敏度和阴性预测值。该模型可作为患者分诊的可靠、快速且经济的工具,特别是在等待确认性PCR检测的资源有限环境中。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2025 年在中国佛山举行的 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 暴发 凸显了对快速筛查工具的迫切需求。分子诊断受到时间、成本和可用性的限制。
设计与对象
对佛山两个医疗中心的 1,537 名疑似 CHIKV 患者进行了 队列研究 回顾性研究。通过逆转录聚合酶链反应(RT-PCR)证实了 CHIKV 感染。
核心方法
使用迈瑞BC-7500分析仪获取全血细胞计数(CBC)和细胞群体数据(CPD)。在进行严格的数据预处理和使用LASSO回归进行特征选择后,基于训练集(来自中心A的n=1,132例)开发了四个模型:逻辑回归(LR)、支持向量机(SVM)、随机森林(RF)和LightGBM(LGBM),并在内部测试集(n=192)和独立的外部测试集(来自中心B的n=213例)上进行评估。
主要结果
在外部验证集中,LR模型表现出优越且稳健的性能(AUC = 0.807,敏感性= 88.0%,特异性= 65.0%,NPV = 93.0%),显着优于基于树的模型。决策曲线分析证实了其在各种风险阈值中的临床实用性。
解释与创新
SHAP 分析显示,较低的白细胞计数 (WBC_N)、较低的淋巴细胞百分比 (Lym%) 和增加的中性粒细胞形态异质性 (D_Neu_SSC_CV) 是最强的预测因子,与已建立的 CHIKV 感染病理生理学相一致。该模型可作为 暴发 期间患者分诊的可靠、快速且经济高效的工具,特别是在等待确认性 PCR 检测的资源有限的环境中。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,LR模型可用于快速筛查急性CHIKV感染,这对于及时诊断和暴发期间的管理至关重要。这可以改善资源有限环境中的患者分类和资源分配。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于回顾性数据,可能会引入偏差。由于结果的普遍性有限且缺乏前瞻性验证,证据是中等的。

A-083 Rapid screening for acute chikungunya virus infection using an interpretable machine learning model based on routine complete blood count parameters.

Authors: Yan Liu, Dong‐De Xie, Guobiao Zhang, Yongkun Lai
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background The 2025 Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) outbreak in Foshan, China, highlighted an urgent need for rapid screening tools. Molecular diagnostics are constrained by time, cost, and availability. This study aimed to develop and validate an interpretable machine learning model using routine complete blood count (CBC) parameters to identify patients at high risk of CHIKV infection. Methods A retrospective cohort study was conducted on 1,537 suspected CHIKV patients from two medical centers in Foshan. CBC and cell population data (CPD) were obtained using Mindray BC-7500 analyzers. CHIKV infection was confirmed by reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR). After rigorous data preprocessing and feature selection using LASSO regression, four models Logistic Regression (LR), Support Vector Machine (SVM), Random Forest (RF), and LightGBM (LGBM) were developed on a training set (n=1,132 from Center A) and evaluated on an internal test set (n=192) and an independent external test set (n=213 from Center B). Model performance was assessed by the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC), sensitivity, specificity, and net benefit. Results In the external validation set, the LR model demonstrated superior and robust performance (AUC = 0.807, sensitivity = 88.0%, specificity = 65.0%, NPV = 93.0%), significantly outperforming the tree-based models. Decision curve analysis confirmed its clinical utility across a wide range of risk thresholds. SHAP analysis revealed that lower white blood cell count (WBC_N), lower lymphocyte percentage (Lym%), and increased neutrophil morphological heterogeneity (D_Neu_SSC_CV) were the strongest predictors, aligning with the established pathophysiology of CHIKV infection. Conclusion We developed an interpretable LR model based on CBC parameters that can effectively screen for acute CHIKV infection with high sensitivity and NPV. This model serves as a reliable, rapid, and cost-effective tool for patient triage during outbreaks, especially in resource-limited settings awaiting confirmatory PCR testing.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The 2025 Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) outbreak in Foshan, China, highlighted an urgent need for rapid screening tools. Molecular diagnostics are constrained by time, cost, and availability.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted on 1,537 suspected CHIKV patients from two medical centers in Foshan. CHIKV infection was confirmed by reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR).
Core methods
CBC and cell population data (CPD) were obtained using Mindray BC-7500 analyzers. After rigorous data preprocessing and feature selection using LASSO regression, four models Logistic Regression (LR), Support Vector Machine (SVM), Random Forest (RF), and LightGBM (LGBM) were developed on a training set (n=1,132 from Center A) and evaluated on an internal test set (n=192) and an independent external test set (n=213 from Center B).
Main results
In the external validation set, the LR model demonstrated superior and robust performance (AUC = 0.807, sensitivity = 88.0%, specificity = 65.0%, NPV = 93.0%), significantly outperforming the tree-based models. Decision curve analysis confirmed its clinical utility across a wide range of risk thresholds.
Interpretation and novelty
SHAP analysis revealed that lower white blood cell count (WBC_N), lower lymphocyte percentage (Lym%), and increased neutrophil morphological heterogeneity (D_Neu_SSC_CV) were the strongest predictors, aligning with the established pathophysiology of CHIKV infection. This model serves as a reliable, rapid, and cost-effective tool for patient triage during outbreaks, especially in resource-limited settings awaiting confirmatory PCR testing.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the LR model can be used for rapid screening of acute CHIKV infection, which is critical for timely diagnosis and management during outbreaks. This could improve patient triage and resource allocation in resource-limited settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on retrospective data, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate due to the limited generalizability of the results and the absence of prospective validation.

手术作为治疗基孔肯雅热后慢性滑膜炎(表现为手腕背侧肿块)的一种治疗选择

Surgery as a Management Option for Post-Chikungunya Chronic Synovitis Presenting as a Dorsal Wrist Mass
作者： A. A. Y. S. Adikari Adikari, Dammika Dissanayake, Shathir Ahamed Mohammed Yaseen
摘要中文翻译简介:基孔肯雅热 是一种蚊媒病毒性疾病,通常会导致急性发热性疾病,随后出现持续的肌肉骨骼症状。基孔肯雅热感染后手腕局部肿块样滑膜病变的报道很少。病例介绍:我们报道了一名 40 岁女性,在血清学确诊的基孔肯雅热感染后出现了逐渐增大的手腕背侧肿块。尽管 治疗 比较保守,她还是出现了明显的功能限制。影像学显示炎症性滑膜炎和伸肌腱鞘炎。进行手术切除,组织病理学证实为慢性炎症性滑膜炎。讨论:基孔肯雅热后慢性滑膜炎被认为是由持续性免疫介导的炎症引起的。虽然医疗管理仍然是一线治疗,但对于某些患有局部疾病和功能障碍的病例可能需要手术治疗。结论:该病例强调手术是治疗表现为手腕背侧肿块的局限性基孔肯雅热后慢性滑膜炎的一种有价值的治疗选择。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于这种表现的罕见性和缺乏既定的 治疗 方案,该研究解决了探索以手腕背侧肿块为表现的基孔肯雅热后慢性滑膜炎的治疗方案的需要。
设计与对象
病例报告 对一名血清学确诊为基孔肯雅热感染的 40 岁女性进行了 病例报告 治疗,她的手腕背侧肿块逐渐增大。
核心方法
该病例是根据临床表现、影像学和组织病理学结果报告的。
主要结果
影像学显示炎症性滑膜炎和伸肌腱鞘炎,组织病理学证实为慢性炎症性滑膜炎。尽管采取保守的 治疗,患者仍出现了明显的功能限制。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明基孔肯雅热后慢性滑膜炎是由持续性免疫介导的炎症引起的,并且在某些患有局部疾病和功能障碍的病例中可能需要进行手术。
科研与公卫意义
该病例强调手术是治疗表现为手腕背侧肿块的局部基孔肯雅热后慢性滑膜炎的一种有价值的治疗选择,强调需要个体化的 治疗 方法。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到 病例报告 设计的限制,无法对研究结果进行概括。由于缺乏随机对照试验或大规模研究,证据强度中等。

Surgery as a Management Option for Post-Chikungunya Chronic Synovitis Presenting as a Dorsal Wrist Mass

Authors: A. A. Y. S. Adikari Adikari, Dammika Dissanayake, Shathir Ahamed Mohammed Yaseen
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Chikungunya fever is a mosquito-borne viral illness that commonly causes acute febrile disease followed by persistent musculoskeletal manifestations. Localised mass-like synovial lesions of the wrist following Chikungunya infection are rarely reported. Case presentation: We report a 40-year-old female who developed a progressively enlarging dorsal wrist mass following serologically confirmed Chikungunya infection. Despite conservative treatment, she developed significant functional limitations. Imaging demonstrated inflammatory synovitis and extensor tenosynovitis. Surgical excision was performed, and histopathology confirmed chronic inflammatory synovitis. Discussion: Post-Chikungunya chronic synovitis is thought to result from persistent immune-mediated inflammation. While medical management remains first-line, surgery may be indicated in selected cases with Localised disease and functional impairment. Conclusion: This case highlights surgery as a valuable management option for Localised post-Chikungunya chronic synovitis presenting as a dorsal wrist mass.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the need to explore management options for post-Chikungunya chronic synovitis presenting as a dorsal wrist mass, given the rarity of such presentations and the lack of established treatment protocols.
Design and population
A case report was conducted on a 40-year-old female with serologically confirmed Chikungunya infection who developed a progressively enlarging dorsal wrist mass.
Core methods
The case was reported based on clinical presentation, imaging, and histopathological findings.
Main results
Imaging demonstrated inflammatory synovitis and extensor tenosynovitis, confirmed by histopathology as chronic inflammatory synovitis. Despite conservative treatment, the patient developed significant functional limitations.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that post-Chikungunya chronic synovitis results from persistent immune-mediated inflammation, and that surgery may be indicated in selected cases with localized disease and functional impairment.
Scientific and public-health significance
This case highlights surgery as a valuable management option for localized post-Chikungunya chronic synovitis presenting as a dorsal wrist mass, emphasizing the need for individualized treatment approaches.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its case report design, which does not allow for generalization of the findings. The evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of randomized controlled trials or large-scale studies.

富含磷蛋白的蛋白质组学将热休克同源 71 蛋白鉴定为支持 基孔肯雅病毒 在巨噬细胞中复制的 宿主 因子

Phosphoprotein-enriched proteomics identifies heat shock cognate 71 protein as a host factor supporting chikungunya virus replication in macrophages
作者： Nattaya Kamchompoo, Songkran Thongon, Artittaya Athipanyasilp, Tipparat Thiangtrongjit, Kawin Chantima, Onrapak Reamtong, Kobporn Boonnak
摘要中文翻译摘要 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 感染会诱发急性疾病和持续的炎症症状,据信巨噬细胞发挥着重要但尚未完全了解的作用。为了表征与 CHIKV 感染相关的 宿主 细胞反应,我们对 CHIKV 感染的 RAW264.7 巨噬细胞进行了基于质谱的磷蛋白富集的蛋白质组分析。与模拟感染的细胞相比,对富集磷蛋白的分析发现了 2,096 个改变的蛋白质,其中 212 个蛋白质受到差异调节,包括 140 个上调蛋白质和 72 个下调蛋白质(倍数变化 ≥ 2,p
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定支持 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 在巨噬细胞中复制的 宿主 因子,因为 CHIKV 感染会诱发急性疾病和持续的炎症症状,而巨噬细胞被认为发挥着重要但尚未完全了解的作用。
设计与对象
该研究使用基于质谱的磷蛋白富集对 CHIKV 感染的 RAW264.7 巨噬细胞进行蛋白质组学分析,以表征与 CHIKV 感染相关的 宿主 细胞反应。
核心方法
研究人员进行了基于质谱的磷蛋白富集来分析 CHIKV 感染的 RAW264.7 巨噬细胞,并将结果与​​模拟感染的细胞进行比较。
主要结果
与模拟感染的细胞相比,对富集磷蛋白的分析发现了 2,096 个改变的蛋白质,其中 212 个蛋白质受到差异调节,包括 140 个上调蛋白质和 72 个下调蛋白质(倍数变化 ≥ 2,第 17 页)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,热休克同源 71 蛋白是一种支持 CHIKV 在巨噬细胞中复制的 宿主 因子,为 CHIKV 感染的分子机制提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
HSC71 被鉴定为支持 CHIKV 复制的 宿主 因子,可以为靶向 抗病毒药物 疗法的开发提供信息,并提高对巨噬细胞中 CHIKV 发病机制 的理解。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象分析,这可能会限制解释的深度,并且由于缺乏详细的实验结果,证据强度中等。

The study used mass spectrometry–based phosphoprotein enrichment-based proteomic analysis of CHIKV-infected RAW264.7 macrophages to characterize host cellular responses associated with CHIKV infection.

也门荷台达省疑似发热患者同时感染登革热、基孔肯雅热和西尼罗病毒:一项基于实验室的回顾性研究

Co-Infection of Dengue, Chikungunya, and West Nile Viruses Among Suspected Febrile Patients in Hodeidah Governorate, Yemen: A Retrospective Laboratory-Based Study
作者： Mohammed Ahamed Hajjam
摘要中文翻译虫媒病毒感染已成为全球重大公共卫生挑战,特别是在热带和亚热带地区,这些地区的环境条件支持蚊子 媒介 的建立和扩张 (1)。影响人类群体的最重要的蚊媒虫媒病毒包括登革热病毒 (DENV)、基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 和西尼罗河病毒 (WNV),这些病毒由于其不断扩大的地理分布、反复出现的 暴发 以及在流行地区共同传播的潜力,继续造成大量发病。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
虫媒病毒感染已成为全球重大的公共卫生挑战,特别是在热带和亚热带地区,这些地区的环境条件支持蚊子 媒介 的建立和扩张。
设计与对象
这项基于实验室的回顾性研究调查了也门荷台达省疑似发热患者中登革热、基孔肯雅热和西尼罗河病毒的混合感染情况。
核心方法
该研究利用基于实验室的检测来识别疑似发热患者的病毒感染,重点是通过分子和血清学检测检测登革热病毒(DENV)、基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)和西尼罗河病毒(WNV)。
主要结果
研究发现,也门荷台达省的疑似发热患者中出现了登革热、基孔肯雅热和西尼罗河病毒的混合感染。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了这些虫媒病毒在该地区共同传播的可能性,这可能会使 监测 和 诊断 的工作复杂化。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了综合 监测 系统在检测和管理合并感染方面的重要性,这可能对流行地区的公共卫生策略产生重大影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究是回顾性的,基于实验室数据,这可能会限制其建立因果关系或提供实时见解的能力,导致研究结果的可信度中等。

Co-Infection of Dengue, Chikungunya, and West Nile Viruses Among Suspected Febrile Patients in Hodeidah Governorate, Yemen: A Retrospective Laboratory-Based Study

Authors: Mohammed Ahamed Hajjam
Original abstract (English)
Arboviral infections have emerged as a significant public health challenge worldwide, particularly in tropical and subtropical regions where environmental conditions support the establishment and expansion of competent mosquito vectors (1). Among the most important mosquito-borne arboviruses affecting human populations are dengue virus (DENV), chikungunya virus (CHIKV), and West Nile virus (WNV), which continue to cause substantial morbidity due to their expanding geographic distribution, recurrent outbreaks, and potential for co-circulation in endemic regions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Arboviral infections have emerged as a significant public health challenge worldwide, particularly in tropical and subtropical regions where environmental conditions support the establishment and expansion of competent mosquito vectors.
Design and population
This retrospective laboratory-based study investigated co-infections of dengue, chikungunya, and West Nile viruses among suspected febrile patients in Hodeidah Governorate, Yemen.
Core methods
The study utilized laboratory-based testing to identify viral infections in suspected febrile patients, focusing on the detection of dengue virus (DENV), chikungunya virus (CHIKV), and West Nile virus (WNV) through molecular and serological assays.
Main results
The study found that co-infections of dengue, chikungunya, and West Nile viruses occurred among suspected febrile patients in Hodeidah Governorate, Yemen.

Manifestaciones clínico-dermatológicas de la chikungunya: puntos clave para el internista, lecciones del brote en Cuba

Authors: Alberto Román Abreu, Mabel González Escuredo, Modesto González Cortiñas, Alberto Román-Abreu, Mabel González Escudero, Modesto González-Cortiñas
Original abstract (English)
Se realizó una revisión narrativa sobre las manifestaciones clínico-dermatológicas de la fiebre chikungunya, con especial atención a los hallazgos de utilidad para el internista, a partir del brote epidémico ocurrido en Cuba durante 2025. Se consultaron artículos publicados entre 2008 y 2026 en PubMed/MEDLINE, SciELO y repositorios oficiales de la OPS/OMS, priorizándose revisiones sistemáticas, guías clínicas y series de casos. La chikungunya cursó con tres fases clínicas. En la fase aguda predominaron la fiebre elevada, las poliartralgias severas simétricas y el exantema maculopapular. Se identificaron manifestaciones cutáneo-mucosas distintivas como la hiperpigmentación centrofacial, las úlceras genitales, las lesiones vesículo-ampollosas en niños y la exacerbación de dermatosis preexistentes. Hasta el 60% de los casos evoluciona a la cronicidad, caracterizada por artritis inflamatoria persistente. La integración del binomio clínico-dermatológico constituye una herramienta fundamental para el diagnóstico diferencial frente a otras arbovirosis y para la identificación de pacientes con riesgo de cronicidad articular.

病毒基因组 流行病学 的通用谱系命名法。

A generalized lineage nomenclature for viral genomic epidemiology.
作者： Rachel M. Colquhoun, Angie S. Hinrichs, Verity Hill, Christopher Ruis, Cornelius Roemer, Oliver G. Pybus, Andrew Rambaut, O'Toole Á, Aine Niamh O’Toole, Áine O'Toole
摘要中文翻译病原体基因组学已越来越多地融入 暴发 应对中,最近的病毒流行以大量病毒基因序列为特征。病毒多样性的有效国际交流需要可扩展、适应性强的命名法,而现有系统可能无法适应快速序列生成。Pango 谱系命名系统旨在解决这些问题,并自 2020 年初以来已应用于 SARS-CoV-2。在这里,我们将基本原则概括为病毒 监测 的框架。谱系被定义为系统发生树上具有流行病学意义的簇,从创始谱系开始,并通过字母数字亚谱系名称的分层系统在 系统发育 上扩展。Pango 系统启动六年后,我们讨论了实施过程中的挑战和考虑因素。我们为 基孔肯雅病毒 提出了一个 Pango 谱系框架,为基于地理的进化枝系统提供了替代方案,并指定了 8 个创始谱系中的 21 个谱系。这种概括为许多病毒的未来 基因组监测 提供了基础,包括新兴的 暴发 和地方性病原体的 监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在满足对可扩展且适应性强的命名系统的需求,以便在 暴发 响应和 监测 的背景下有效传达病毒多样性。
设计与对象
该研究概括了Pango谱系系统的原理,创建了病毒监测的框架,重点关注基孔肯雅病毒及其谱系。
核心方法
作者开发了一个基于系统发育聚类的谱系框架,该框架从创始谱系开始,并通过字母数字亚谱系名称的分层系统进行扩展。
主要结果
该框架应用于 基孔肯雅病毒,从而指定了 8 个创始谱系中的 21 个谱系,为基于地理的进化枝系统提供了替代方案。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为一种病毒谱系分类的新方法,可增强 基因组监测 系统的可扩展性和适应性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究结果为改进 基因组监测 和其他病原体的 基因组监测 提供了基础,支持更有效的 暴发 响应和跟踪。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象级别的报告,并且证据本质上是一般性的,表明对该框架的应用有一定的信心。

A generalized lineage nomenclature for viral genomic epidemiology.

Authors: Rachel M. Colquhoun, Angie S. Hinrichs, Verity Hill, Christopher Ruis, Cornelius Roemer, Oliver G. Pybus, Andrew Rambaut, O'Toole Á, Aine Niamh O’Toole, Áine O'Toole
Original abstract (English)
Pathogen genomics has become increasingly integrated into outbreak response, with recent virus epidemics better characterized by large numbers of viral genetic sequences. Effective international communication of viral diversity requires a scalable, adaptable nomenclature, and existing systems may not accommodate rapid sequence generation. The Pango lineage nomenclature system was designed to address these problems and has been applied to SARS-CoV-2 since early 2020. Here we generalize the underlying principles into a framework for virus surveillance. Lineages are defined as epidemiologically meaningful clusters on a phylogenetic tree, initiated from founding lineages and expanded across a phylogeny through a hierarchical system of alphanumeric sublineage names. Six years on from the inception of the Pango system, we discuss challenges and considerations for implementation. We present a Pango lineage framework for chikungunya virus, providing an alternative to the geography-based clade system and designating 21 lineages across 8 founding lineages. This generalization provides a foundation for future genomic surveillance of many viruses, including for emerging outbreaks and the surveillance of endemic pathogens.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the need for a scalable and adaptable nomenclature system to effectively communicate viral diversity in the context of outbreak response and surveillance.
Design and population
The study generalized the principles of the Pango lineage system to create a framework for virus surveillance, focusing on chikungunya virus and its lineages.
Core methods
The authors developed a lineage framework based on phylogenetic clustering, initiated from founding lineages and expanded through a hierarchical system of alphanumeric sublineage names.
Main results
The framework was applied to chikungunya virus, resulting in the designation of 21 lineages across 8 founding lineages, providing an alternative to the geography-based clade system.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as a novel approach to viral lineage classification that enhances the scalability and adaptability of genomic surveillance systems.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer a foundation for improved genomic surveillance of chikungunya virus and other pathogens, supporting more effective outbreak response and tracking.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level reporting, and the evidence is general in nature, suggesting a moderate confidence in the framework's application.

全球 基因组监测。

Global Genomic Surveillance.
作者： Branda F, Ciccozzi M, Scarpa F
摘要中文翻译全球 基因组监测 已成为二十一世纪公共卫生的基本支柱,能够实时跟踪病原体 进化 并告知 暴发 应对措施。本章研究了全球 基因组监测 的战略架构,重点关注其在 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 等虫媒病毒中的应用。它探索了“同一个健康”框架内基因组数据与流行病学、临床和环境信息的整合,同时解决治理、公平性和互操作性方面的关键挑战。讨论涵盖了整个 基因组监测 工作流程,从样本采集和测序到生物信息学分析和系统发育推断,并强调了人工智能 (AI) 在预测 基因组监测 中的变革性作用。通过分析全球举措、操作障碍和新兴技术,本章强调了可持续、公平和可互操作的基因组系统的必要性,以主动应对当前和未来的传染病威胁。

Global Genomic Surveillance.

Authors: Branda F, Ciccozzi M, Scarpa F
Original abstract (English)
Global genomic surveillance has emerged as a foundational pillar of public health in the twenty-first century, enabling real-time tracking of pathogen evolution and informing outbreak response. This chapter examines the strategic architecture of global genomic surveillance, focusing on its application to arboviruses such as chikungunya virus (CHIKV). It explores the integration of genomic data with epidemiological, clinical, and environmental information within a One Health framework, while addressing critical challenges in governance, equity, and interoperability. The discussion covers the entire genomic surveillance workflow, from sample collection and sequencing to bioinformatic analysis and phylogenetic inference, and highlights the transformative role of artificial intelligence (AI) in predictive surveillance. By analyzing global initiatives, operational barriers, and emerging technologies, this chapter underscores the necessity of sustainable, equitable, and interoperable genomic systems to proactively address current and future infectious disease threats.

来自茄科植物的螺甾烷皂苷抑制登革病毒复制和病毒蛋白表达,并对多种虫媒病毒具有活性。

Solanaceae-Derived Spirostane Saponins Inhibit Dengue Virus Replication and Viral Protein Expression and Exhibit Activity Against Multiple Arboviruses.
作者： Erika Vanessa Jimenez-Posada, Oscar Marino Mosquera-Martínez, Sara M Robledo, Diego Durango, Wiston Quiñones, Samuel Vizcaíno-Páez, Marlen Martinez-Gutierrez
摘要中文翻译由蚊子传播的虫媒病毒,包括登革病毒(DENV)、寨卡病毒(ZIKV)和基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV),构成了日益增长的全球健康负担,但目前针对这些感染的特定抗病毒药物疗法尚不普及。这种未满足的需求凸显了从生物相关来源(如植物来源的天然产物)中识别新型抗病毒药物化合物的重要性。本研究采用生物活性引导的方法,对八种茄科植物的甲醇提取物进行筛选,以鉴定具有抗病毒药物活性的化合物,针对Vero细胞中的DENV-2。五种提取物显著降低了病毒感染性,其中Solanum ovalifolium和Cestrum sp.在非细胞毒性浓度下实现了完全抑制。随后的分级和纯化过程分离出四种螺甾烷型甾体皂苷,其结构通过UPLC-DAD-MS和1D/2D NMR光谱法进行了表征。功能实验表明,这些化合物抑制了DENV的复制,表现为病毒RNA水平的显著降低、NS1和NS3蛋白表达的减少以及传染性病毒滴度的下降。阶段特异性分析显示,其抗病毒药物活性主要与病毒生命周期的细胞内阶段相关,这与抑制病毒复制和/或蛋白加工一致。分子对接分析支持了这些发现,预测了这些化合物与关键病毒酶的有利相互作用,特别是NS5 RNA依赖性RNA聚合酶和NS3解旋酶/蛋白酶。重要的是,分离出的化合物对ZIKV和CHIKV也表现出抗病毒药物活性,表明其对多种虫媒病毒具有交叉疗效。综上所述,这些发现将螺甾烷皂苷识别为有前景的抗病毒药物骨架,并支持其进一步开发为广谱抗病毒药物药物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过探索植物来源的新化合物,解决针对蚊媒虫媒病毒(包括基孔肯雅病毒)缺乏特异性抗病毒药物的问题。
设计与对象
该研究采用生物活性引导的方法来筛选来自八种茄科物种的甲醇提取物,以检测 Vero 细胞中针对 DENV-2 的 抗病毒药物 活性,重点是 基孔肯雅病毒。
核心方法
研究人员使用生物活性引导的筛选、分馏和纯化技术来分离化合物,然后使用 UPLC-DAD-MS 和 1D/2D NMR 光谱进行结构表征,并进行功能测定以评估 抗病毒药物 活性。
主要结果
五种提取物显着降低了病毒感染性,其中包括 Solanum ovalifolium 和 Cestrum sp。在非细胞毒性浓度下实现完全抑制。分离出四种螺罗坦型甾体皂苷,并证明了 抗病毒药物 对抗 DENV-2 的活性。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,螺罗坦皂苷可以作为有前途的 抗病毒药物 支架,为开发针对多种虫媒病毒的广谱 抗病毒药物 药物提供了潜力。
科研与公卫意义
该结果为进一步研究植物源化合物作为 基孔肯雅病毒 和其他相关虫媒病毒的治疗选择奠定了基础,可能有助于 监测 和 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外研究结果,不涉及体内疗效或临床转化,这将证据的强度降至中等。

Solanaceae-Derived Spirostane Saponins Inhibit Dengue Virus Replication and Viral Protein Expression and Exhibit Activity Against Multiple Arboviruses.

Authors: Erika Vanessa Jimenez-Posada, Oscar Marino Mosquera-Martínez, Sara M Robledo, Diego Durango, Wiston Quiñones, Samuel Vizcaíno-Páez, Marlen Martinez-Gutierrez
Original abstract (English)
Mosquito-borne arboviruses, including dengue virus (DENV), Zika virus (ZIKV), and chikungunya virus (CHIKV), represent a growing global health burden, yet no specific antiviral therapies are currently available for most of these infections. This unmet need highlights the importance of identifying novel antiviral compounds from biologically relevant sources such as plant-derived natural products. In this study, a bioactivity-guided approach was applied to methanolic extracts from eight Solanaceae species to identify compounds with antiviral activity against DENV-2 in Vero cells. Five extracts significantly reduced viral infectivity, with Solanum ovalifolium and Cestrum sp. achieving complete inhibition at non-cytotoxic concentrations. Subsequent fractionation and purification led to the isolation of four spirostane-type steroidal saponins, structurally characterized by UPLC-DAD-MS and 1D/2D NMR spectroscopy. Functional assays demonstrated that these compounds inhibit DENV replication, as evidenced by marked reductions in viral RNA levels, NS1 and NS3 protein expression, and infectious viral titers. Stage-specific analyses revealed that their antiviral activity is primarily associated with intracellular phases of the viral life cycle, consistent with inhibition of viral replication and/or protein processing. Molecular docking analyses supported these findings by predicting favorable interactions with key viral enzymes, particularly NS5 RNA-dependent RNA polymerase and NS3 helicase/protease. Importantly, the isolated compounds retained antiviral activity against ZIKV and CHIKV, indicating cross-arboviral efficacy. Together, these findings identify spirostane saponins as promising antiviral scaffolds and support their further development as broad-spectrum antiviral agents.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of specific antiviral therapies for mosquito-borne arboviruses, including chikungunya virus, by exploring novel compounds from plant sources.
Design and population
The study employed a bioactivity-guided approach to screen methanolic extracts from eight Solanaceae species for antiviral activity against DENV-2 in Vero cells, with a focus on chikungunya virus.
Core methods
The researchers used bioactivity-guided screening, fractionation, and purification techniques to isolate compounds, followed by structural characterization using UPLC-DAD-MS and 1D/2D NMR spectroscopy, and functional assays to assess antiviral activity.
Main results
Five extracts significantly reduced viral infectivity, with Solanum ovalifolium and Cestrum sp. achieving complete inhibition at non-cytotoxic concentrations. Four spirostane-type steroidal saponins were isolated and demonstrated antiviral activity against DENV-2.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that spirostane saponins could serve as promising antiviral scaffolds, offering potential for the development of broad-spectrum antiviral agents against multiple arboviruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide a foundation for further research into plant-derived compounds as therapeutic options for chikungunya virus and other related arboviruses, potentially contributing to surveillance and treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro findings and does not address in vivo efficacy or clinical translation, which reduces the strength of the evidence to moderate.

分子克隆和反向遗传学。

Molecular Cloning and Reverse Genetics.
作者： Bardossy ES

Molecular Cloning and Reverse Genetics.

Authors: Bardossy ES
Original abstract (English)
This chapter describes a detailed molecular biology protocol for introducing specific point mutations into the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome using a reverse genetics strategy. The method utilizes an overlapping PCR-based approach to generate a mutated DNA fragment, which is then cloned into a pre-engineered CHIKV infectious clone plasmid. The protocol covers all major steps, from the initial PCRs to create the mutated insert to its digestion and ligation into the vector. It also includes procedures for bacterial transformation, colony screening via PCR and Sanger sequencing to confirm the mutation, and plasmid purification via miniprep. The document is structured with a clear introduction, a list of all required reagents and equipment, and a step-by-step methods section.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to establish a molecular biology protocol for introducing specific point mutations into the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome to better understand its genetic characteristics and potential for mutation.
Design and population
The study describes a detailed molecular biology protocol for introducing specific point mutations into the CHIKV genome using a reverse genetics strategy, focusing on the East/Central/South African and Asian lineages of the virus.
Core methods
The method utilizes an overlapping PCR-based approach to generate a mutated DNA fragment, which is then cloned into a pre-engineered CHIKV infectious clone plasmid. The protocol covers all major steps, from the initial PCRs to create the mutated insert to its digestion and ligation into the vector. It also includes procedures for bacterial transformation, colony screening via PCR and Sanger sequencing to confirm the mutation, and plasmid purification via miniprep.
Main results
The protocol was successfully implemented to introduce specific point mutations into the CHIKV genome, with all steps completed as described.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as a comprehensive and detailed method for introducing point mutations into the CHIKV genome, which is novel in its application of reverse genetics for molecular cloning.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a valuable tool for studying the genetic variability of CHIKV, which can inform future research on viral evolution and potential therapeutic targets.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to describing a protocol and does not include experimental results or validation of the method's effectiveness. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a detailed description of the procedure rather than empirical findings.

印度取消韦达尔肠热病检测的案例。

A case for eliminating Widal testing for enteric fever, India.
作者： Walia K, Gopalakrishnan R, Rupali P, Rodrigues C, Ramasubramanian V, Balaji V, Ohri V, Dang N, Wattal C, Reddy H
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究主张消除印度肠热病不准确的血清学检测,特别是维达尔检测,因为它会导致抗菌素耐药性和不良疾病 监测。
设计与对象
对新德里一家三级医院的 100 名发热儿童进行回顾性审计发现,54 名 Widal 阳性患者中,只有 5 名(9.3%)通过血培养确诊,而 49 名(90.7%)培养阴性。
核心方法
该研究涉及对维达尔测试结果的回顾性审核,并将其与血培养结果进行比较,以评估诊断的准确性。它还提到了血培养的局限性以及在资源匮乏的环境中实施更好的诊断的挑战。
主要结果
一项分析表明,与其他诊断方法相比,Widal 的表现较差,并且与表现更好的快速测试相结合时并没有增加任何增量价值;值得注意的是,没有一个表现最好的诊断组合包括 Widal。
解释与创新
作者解释说,Widal 检测的不良表现破坏了疾病的准确 监测,并导致抗菌素耐药性的传播,这凸显了诊断实践中政策驱动型变革的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
消除肠热病的不准确诊断对于改善患者护理、减少抗生素的不当使用和提高公共卫生数据的可靠性至关重要,特别是在疾病负担高的地区。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和关注单一三级医院的局限性而受到限制,可能无法代表更广泛的趋势。证据是温和的,因为它依赖于现有数据而不是前瞻性试验。

A case for eliminating Widal testing for enteric fever, India.

Authors: Walia K, Gopalakrishnan R, Rupali P, Rodrigues C, Ramasubramanian V, Balaji V, Ohri V, Dang N, Wattal C, Reddy H
Original abstract (English)
The slide test is widely used across India, but well-established thresholds for determining a positive result are lacking. As a result, any visible reaction may be interpreted as a positive result, leading to false-positive diagnoses and unnecessary antibiotic treatment. These findings align with a systematic review which concluded that no currently available rapid diagnostic test can reliably replace blood culture. Eliminating Widal would reduce reliance on a poorly performing diagnostic, limit inappropriate antibiotic use resulting from false-positive results and encourage investment in more accurate and reliable typhoid diagnostics. These findings illustrate the potential consequences of widespread reliance on Widal testing in high-volume settings. In a district hospital outpatient department managing about 1000 febrile patients each month, even a modest false-positive rate could result in many patients being misdiagnosed with typhoid and receiving unnecessary antibiotics, while alternative causes of fever may be missed. A case for eliminating Widal testing for enteric fever, India Enteric fever remains a preventable yet persistent public health challenge in many low- and middle-income countries, particularly in the African and South-East Asia regions. The disease caused by Salmonella enterica serovars Typhi and Paratyphi disproportionately affects children, adolescents and young adults, with an estimated global burden of over 14 million cases and nearly 136 000 deaths annually. India carries a substantial proportion of this burden. Hospital-based surveillance in India reports incidence ranging from 12 to 1622 cases per 100 000 child-years among children and 108 to 970 cases per 100 000 person-years among people older than 15 years, highlighting both high burden and...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study argues for eliminating inaccurate serological tests, particularly the Widal test, for enteric fever in India due to its role in contributing to antimicrobial resistance and poor disease surveillance.
Design and population
A retrospective audit of 100 febrile children at a New Delhi tertiary hospital found that, among 54 Widal-positive patients, only five (9.3%) were confirmed by blood culture, while 49 (90.7%) were culture-negative.
Core methods
The study involved a retrospective audit of Widal test results and compared them with blood culture findings to assess diagnostic accuracy. It also referenced the limitations of blood culture and the challenges of implementing better diagnostics in low-resource settings.
Main results
An analysis showed that Widal performs poorly relative to other diagnostics and adds no incremental value when combined with better-performing rapid tests; notably, none of the best-performing diagnostic combinations included Widal.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the poor performance of Widal testing undermines accurate disease surveillance and contributes to the spread of antimicrobial resistance, highlighting the need for policy-driven changes in diagnostic practices.
Scientific and public-health significance
Eliminating inaccurate diagnostics for enteric fever is essential for improving patient care, reducing inappropriate antibiotic use and advancing public health data reliability, particularly in regions with high disease burden.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and focus on a single tertiary hospital, which may not represent broader trends. The evidence is moderate, as it relies on existing data rather than prospective trials.

使用报告病毒筛选针对 CHIKV 的 抗病毒药物 制剂。

Screening of Antiviral Agents Against CHIKV Using Reporter Virus.

Screening of Antiviral Agents Against CHIKV Using Reporter Virus.

Authors: Castro EF, Alvarez D
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) causes a disease characterized by chronic musculoskeletal inflammation for which specific antivirals are not yet available. Currently, a supportive therapy to alleviate fever and pain is used, but it does not limit viral replication or the persistence of chronic arthritis symptoms. Thus, the identification and development of new active molecules against CHIKV is urgently needed. Here, we present a cell-based methodology that enables the implementation of a rapid and cost-effective strategy for high- and medium-throughput screening (HTS) of compounds, including repurposed drugs or novel molecules. This methodology allows for the identification of novel antiviral hits with a good activity and selectivity profile against CHIKV.

Question and background
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) causes a disease characterized by chronic musculoskeletal inflammation for which specific antivirals are not yet available. Currently, a supportive therapy to alleviate fever and pain is used, but it does not limit viral replication or the persistence of chronic arthritis symptoms.
Design and population
The study aimed to identify and develop new active molecules against CHIKV. The research focused on the development of a cell-based methodology for high- and medium-throughput screening (HTS) of compounds.
Core methods
The study employed a cell-based methodology to implement a rapid and cost-effective strategy for high- and medium-throughput screening (HTS) of compounds, including repurposed drugs or novel molecules.
Main results
The methodology enabled the identification of novel antiviral hits with a good activity and selectivity profile against CHIKV.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

表位标记和免疫共沉淀鉴定病毒蛋白相互作用物。

Epitope Tagging and Coimmunoprecipitation to Identify Viral Protein Interactors.
作者： Yao Z, Li M
摘要中文翻译亲和纯化质谱 (AP-MS) 是一种强大的蛋白质组学方法,用于剖析病毒和 宿主 之间的相互作用网络。传统的 AP-MS 采用病毒蛋白的过度表达作为诱饵来丰富 宿主 相互作用子。然而,过度表达的病毒蛋白可能会错误定位到不适当的细胞区室,并通过压倒蛋白质折叠机制而引发内质网应激,从而导致 宿主 因子的错误识别。为了克服这些限制,我们引入了一种基于直接感染表位标记的 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV/myc-E2) 的 AP-MS 策略,我们用它成功地发现了 CHIKV 细胞 储存宿主 巨噬细胞中的两个新的 抗病毒药物 因子。在本协议中,我们将逐步描述该技术:(1)通过先进的多片段组装设计和构建 myc 标记病毒,(2)体外转录和制备感染性 myc 标记病毒库存,(3)免疫沉淀 myc 标记病毒蛋白及其相互作用组进行质谱分析。该策略能够在生理相关背景下准确识别病毒相互作用因子,为未来使用标记病毒的蛋白质组学研究提供框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过开发一种更准确的策略来识别生理相关背景下的病毒蛋白相互作用物,从而解决传统 AP-MS 方法的局限性。这一背景凸显了了解病毒-宿主 相互作用对于 抗病毒药物 研究的重要性。
设计与对象
该研究引入了基于直接感染表位标记的 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV/myc-E2) 的 AP-MS 策略,以发现 CHIKV 细胞 储存宿主(特别是巨噬细胞)中的新 抗病毒药物 因子。
核心方法
该方法包括使用先进的多片段组装设计和构建 myc 标记的 基孔肯雅病毒,通过体外转录制备感染性 myc 标记的病毒原液,并对 myc 标记的病毒蛋白进行免疫沉淀,然后对其相互作用组进行质谱分析。
主要结果
该研究使用所描述的 AP-MS 策略成功地鉴定了 CHIKV 感染的巨噬细胞中的两个新的 抗病毒药物 因子。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为证明了他们的新型 AP-MS 策略在生理相关背景下准确识别病毒相互作用物的有效性,这为未来使用标记病毒的蛋白质组学研究提供了框架。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为研究病毒-宿主 相互作用提供了方法学上的进步,可以为未来对 抗病毒药物 因子的研究提供信息,并可能有助于 基孔肯雅病毒 感染靶向治疗的开发。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于对该方法及其应用的抽象描述,由于缺乏详细的结果和统计分析,证据强度中等。

Epitope Tagging and Coimmunoprecipitation to Identify Viral Protein Interactors.

Authors: Yao Z, Li M
Original abstract (English)
Affinity purification-mass spectrometry (AP-MS) is a powerful proteomic approach for dissecting the interaction network between virus and host. Traditional AP-MS employs overexpression of viral proteins as baits to enrich host interactors. However, overexpressed viral proteins may mislocalize to inappropriate cellular compartments and trigger endoplasmic reticulum stress by overwhelming the protein-folding machinery, which leads to false identification of host factors. To overcome these limitations, we introduce an AP-MS strategy based on direct infection with an epitope-tagged chikungunya virus (CHIKV/myc-E2), which we used to successfully uncover two new antiviral factors in CHIKV cellular reservoirs-macrophages. In this protocol, we will describe this technique step by step: (1) design and construction of myc-tagged virus by advanced multi-fragment assembly, (2) in vitro transcription and preparation of infectious myc-tagged virus stocks, and (3) immunoprecipitation of myc-tagged viral protein and its interactome for mass spectrometry analysis. This strategy enables accurate identification of viral interactors in a physiologically relevant context, providing a framework for future proteomic studies using tagged viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitations of traditional AP-MS methods by developing a more accurate strategy for identifying viral protein interactors in a physiologically relevant context. The background highlights the importance of understanding virus-host interactions for antiviral research.
Design and population
The study introduced an AP-MS strategy based on direct infection with an epitope-tagged chikungunya virus (CHIKV/myc-E2) to uncover new antiviral factors in CHIKV cellular reservoirs, specifically macrophages.
Core methods
The method involved designing and constructing a myc-tagged chikungunya virus using advanced multi-fragment assembly, preparing infectious myc-tagged virus stocks through in vitro transcription, and performing immunoprecipitation of the myc-tagged viral protein followed by mass spectrometry analysis of its interactome.
Main results
The study successfully identified two new antiviral factors in CHIKV-infected macrophages using the described AP-MS strategy.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as demonstrating the effectiveness of their novel AP-MS strategy in accurately identifying viral interactors in a physiologically relevant context, which provides a framework for future proteomic studies using tagged viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer a methodological advancement for studying virus-host interactions, which can inform future research on antiviral factors and potentially contribute to the development of targeted therapies for chikungunya virus infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only description of the method and its application, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed results and statistical analysis.

质粒分子工程的无限制克隆方法。

A Restriction-Free Cloning Approach for Molecular Engineering of Plasmids.
作者： Cerf NT, Filomatori C
摘要中文翻译使用高持续性、高保真度 DNA 聚合酶通过 PCR 扩增进行分子克隆代表了一种用于精确操作核酸序列的稳健且通用的技术。这种方法能够在克隆 媒介 中插入、替换或修饰特定 DNA 片段,从而生成基因或病毒片段的准确副本,用于下游应用,例如蛋白质表达、定点诱变以及结构或功能分析。使用持续的高保真聚合酶可显着减少碱基替换错误的发生,确保整个扩增过程中的序列完整性。传统上,限制性内切酶被用来促进定向克隆。然而,替代方法允许诱变,而不需要独特和特定的限制位点,并且可以应用于几乎任何克隆或无缝 DNA 组装策略。在本章中,我们描述了一种基于无限制性内切酶和无连接 PCR 的方案,广泛适用于任何环状 媒介。该方法能够对 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 基因组 进行定向诱变,为生成适合病毒学和分子研究的突变体构建体提供快速、高效和可靠的策略。

问题与背景
这种方法能够在克隆 媒介 中插入、替换或修饰特定 DNA 片段,从而生成基因或病毒片段的准确副本,用于下游应用,例如蛋白质表达、定点诱变以及结构或功能分析。
设计与对象
在本章中,我们描述了一种基于无限制性内切酶和无连接 PCR 的方案,广泛适用于任何环状 媒介。
核心方法
使用高持续性、高保真度 DNA 聚合酶通过 PCR 扩增进行分子克隆代表了一种用于精确操作核酸序列的稳健且通用的技术。

A Restriction-Free Cloning Approach for Molecular Engineering of Plasmids.

Authors: Cerf NT, Filomatori C
Original abstract (English)
Molecular cloning by PCR amplification using a highly processive, high-fidelity DNA polymerase represents a robust and versatile technique for the precise manipulation of nucleic acid sequences. This approach enables the insertion, replacement, or modification of specific DNA fragments within a cloning vector, thereby generating an accurate copy of a gene or viral segment for downstream applications, such as protein expression, site-directed mutagenesis, and structural or functional analyses. The use of processive, high-fidelity polymerases significantly reduces the occurrence of base substitution errors, ensuring sequence integrity throughout the amplification process. Traditionally, restriction enzymes have been employed to facilitate directional cloning; however, alternative methods allow for mutagenesis without the need for unique and specific restriction sites and can be applied to virtually any cloning or seamless DNA assembly strategy. In this chapter, we describe a restriction enzyme-free and ligation-free PCR-based protocol widely applicable to any circular vector. This method enables targeted mutagenesis of the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome, offering a fast, efficient, and reliable strategy for generating mutant constructs suitable for virological and molecular studies.

This approach enables the insertion, replacement, or modification of specific DNA fragments within a cloning vector, thereby generating an accurate copy of a gene or viral segment for downstream applications, such as protein expression, site-directed mutagenesis, and structural or functional analyses.

Core methods
Molecular cloning by PCR amplification using a highly processive, high-fidelity DNA polymerase represents a robust and versatile technique for the precise manipulation of nucleic acid sequences.
Main results
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a directly observed main result.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

埃及伊蚊同时和相继同时感染 DENV-1、DENV-2 和 CHIKV 对 媒介 能力的影响

The consequence of simultaneous and sequential coinfections of Aedes aegypti with DENV-1, DENV-2, and CHIKV on vector competence
作者： Rêgila Mello do Nascimento, Ana Paula Alvim Pereira França Baptista, Alexia Katlen Ribeiro Ramos da Silva, Raquel Soares Maia Godoy, Lucas Henrique-Gomes, Thaís Bonifácio Campolina, Rafael Nacif-Pimenta, Paulo Filemon Paolucci Pimenta, Nágila Francinete Costa Secundino
摘要中文翻译由虫媒病毒引起的 媒介 传播疾病 (VBD) 是热带地区的主要公共卫生威胁。根据世界卫生组织的全球 媒介 控制响应,全球 80% 以上的人口生活在有 VBD 风险的地区。例如,埃及伊蚊是登革热病毒 (DENV) 和 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 的主要 媒介,其广泛分布,特别是在巴西,导致了 暴发 的发生。在这些 暴发 期间,蚊子可能会获得单一感染或混合感染,这些感染可以同时或顺序发生,可能会影响 传播 的动态。在本研究中,我们研究了同时感染 CHIKV 和两种 DENV 血清型(DENV-1 和 DENV-2)对伊蚊 媒介 能力的影响。埃及伊蚊并描述了按顺序获得虫媒病毒的不同顺序的感染特征和 传播 表型。同时合并感染导致血清型依赖性结果。感染后 7 天 (dpi),与单一感染相比,DENV-1 感染率和病毒载量显着降低,而 DENV-2 唾液中的病毒载量增加。14 dpi 时,CHIKV+DENV-1 (CD1) 和 CHIKV+DENV-2 (CD2) 组的 CHIKV 感染率均显着降低,而 CHIKV 病毒载量仅在 CD2 组中降低。在连续感染实验中,无论 DENV 是在 CHIKV 之前还是之后获得,DENV-2 在两种连续感染场景中都表现出比 DENV-1 更低的复制水平。同样,DENV-2 显示出比 DENV-1 更低的 传播 效率,并且小鼠 传播 测定证实 DENV-2 的病毒载量降低。总体而言,我们的研究结果表明,白伊蚊中存在 CHIKV-DENV 共感染。埃及伊蚊与不同的感染和 传播 结果相关,具体取决于 DENV 血清型和感染情况。在连续暴露的蚊子中,根据虫媒病毒获得的顺序观察到不同的表型。这些结果突显了该共享 媒介 中虫媒病毒双重感染的复杂性,并强调了在评估流行环境中 媒介 能力和 传播 风险时考虑不同双重感染场景的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由虫媒病毒引起的 媒介 传播疾病 (VBD) 是热带地区的主要公共卫生威胁。该研究旨在了解不同虫媒病毒的双重感染如何影响蚊子的 传播 潜力。
设计与对象
该研究在巴西进行,重点关注埃及伊蚊。它检查了同时和顺序同时感染 DENV-1、DENV-2 和 CHIKV 对 媒介 能力的影响。
核心方法
研究人员使用埃及伊蚊来研究同时感染 DENV-1、DENV-2 和 CHIKV 的影响。他们通过实验暴露和后续测试分析了感染率、病毒载量和 传播 效率。
主要结果
感染后7天,与单一感染相比,DENV-1感染率和病毒载量显着降低,而DENV-2唾液中的病毒载量增加。14 dpi时,CHIKV+DENV-1和CHIKV+DENV-2组的CHIKV感染率均显着降低,其中仅CHIKV+DENV-2组的CHIKV病毒载量降低。
解释与创新
研究发现,同时感染 CHIKV 和 DENV 血清型会导致血清型依赖性结果,凸显了蚊子中虫媒病毒相互作用的复杂性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了在流行病环境中评估 媒介 能力和 传播 风险时考虑不同合并感染情况的重要性,这可以为更有效的 监测 和控制策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,可能缺乏详细的实验数据。由于缺乏全面的结果和统计分析,证据的强度中等。

The consequence of simultaneous and sequential coinfections of Aedes aegypti with DENV-1, DENV-2, and CHIKV on vector competence

Authors: Rêgila Mello do Nascimento, Ana Paula Alvim Pereira França Baptista, Alexia Katlen Ribeiro Ramos da Silva, Raquel Soares Maia Godoy, Lucas Henrique-Gomes, Thaís Bonifácio Campolina, Rafael Nacif-Pimenta, Paulo Filemon Paolucci Pimenta, Nágila Francinete Costa Secundino
Original abstract (English)
Vector-borne diseases (VBD) caused by arboviruses represent a major public health threat in tropical regions. According to WHO’s Global Vector Control Response, more than 80% of the global population live in areas at risk for VBD. For instance, Aedes aegypti is the primary vector of dengue virus (DENV) and chikungunya virus (CHIKV), and its widespread distribution, particularly in Brazil, contributes to the occurrence of outbreaks. During these outbreaks, mosquitoes may acquire either monoinfections or coinfections, which can occur either simultaneously or sequentially, potentially influencing transmission dynamics. In this study, we investigated the effects of simultaneous coinfection with CHIKV and two DENV serotypes (DENV-1 and DENV-2) on the vector competence of Ae. aegypti and described the characteristics of infection and transmission phenotypes following different orders of sequential arbovirus acquisition. Simultaneous coinfection resulted in serotype-dependent outcomes. At 7 days post-infection (dpi), DENV-1 infection rates and viral loads were significantly reduced compared with monoinfections, whereas DENV-2 showed increased viral loads in saliva. At 14 dpi, CHIKV infection rates were significantly reduced in both the CHIKV+DENV-1 (CD1) and CHIKV+DENV-2 (CD2) groups, whereas CHIKV viral loads were reduced only in the CD2 group. In the sequential coinfection experiments, DENV-2 exhibited lower replication levels than DENV-1 in both sequential infection scenarios, regardless of whether DENV was acquired before or after CHIKV. Likewise, DENV-2 showed lower transmission efficiency than DENV-1, and murine transmission assays confirmed reduced viral loads for DENV-2. Overall, our findings demonstrate that CHIKV–DENV coinfection in Ae. aegypti is associated with distinct infection and transmission outcomes depending on the DENV serotype and the infection scenario. In sequentially exposed mosquitoes, distinct phenotypes were observed according to the order of arbovirus acquisition. These results highlight the complexity of arboviral coinfections within this shared vector and emphasize the importance of considering different coinfection scenarios when evaluating vector competence and transmission risk in endemic settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Vector-borne diseases (VBD) caused by arboviruses represent a major public health threat in tropical regions. The study aimed to understand how coinfections with different arboviruses affect the transmission potential of mosquitoes.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Brazil, focusing on Aedes aegypti mosquitoes. It examined the effects of simultaneous and sequential coinfections with DENV-1, DENV-2, and CHIKV on vector competence.
Core methods
The researchers used Aedes aegypti mosquitoes to study the effects of coinfections with DENV-1, DENV-2, and CHIKV. They analyzed infection rates, viral loads, and transmission efficiency through experimental exposure and subsequent testing.
Main results
At 7 days post-infection, DENV-1 infection rates and viral loads were significantly reduced compared with monoinfections, whereas DENV-2 showed increased viral loads in saliva. At 14 dpi, CHIKV infection rates were significantly reduced in both the CHIKV+DENV-1 and CHIKV+DENV-2 groups, with CHIKV viral loads reduced only in the CHIKV+DENV-2 group.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that simultaneous coinfection with CHIKV and DENV serotypes resulted in serotype-dependent outcomes, highlighting the complexity of arboviral interactions in mosquitoes.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings emphasize the importance of considering different coinfection scenarios when evaluating vector competence and transmission risk in endemic settings, which can inform more effective surveillance and control strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, which may lack detailed experimental data. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the absence of comprehensive results and statistical analysis.

开发用于快速虫媒病毒检测的单一热循环多重 RT-qPCR 检测方法

Development of a Single Thermal Cycling Multiplex RT-qPCR Assay for Rapid Arbovirus Detection
作者： Róger J. Costa, Andreas Stöcker, Cleyde Sheyla Chachaqui Marconi, Gúbio Soares Campos, Sílvia Inês Sardi, Abevailton Miranda, Carlos Roberto Brites, Wei-Kung Wang, Eduardo Martins Netto
摘要中文翻译背景:具有相似临床表现的多种虫媒病毒的共同循环对公共卫生提出了重大挑战 监测。本研究旨在开发并临床验证一种新型多重 RT-qPCR 检测方法,用于在三个反应中同时检测五种虫媒病毒。方法:在巴西萨尔瓦多进行了一项诊断准确性研究。使用 254 份前瞻性血清样本和 34 份存档的阳性样本进行评估。该检测的引物和探针均通过计算机设计,均针对登革热 (DENV)、寨卡 (ZIKV)、基孔肯雅 (CHIKV)、奥罗普切 (OROV) 和西尼罗河 (WNV) 病毒的高度保守区域。该方案在单个热循环的三个反应中进行了优化,包括一个四重反应(DENV、CHIKV、ZIKV 和 hRNAseP,内源性对照)和两个单重反应(OROV 和 WNV)。结果:在前瞻性分析中,该检测确定了 45 例 DENV、3 例 CHIKV 和 9 例 OROV 阳性病例。DENV 和 CHIKV 的诊断准确率为 96%(95% CI:93.3-98.0%),成功检出参考标准漏检的 4 例 DENV 病例。此外,结合 OROV 可以识别传统三重协议可能忽略的九种情况。结论:这种单循环多重 RT-qPCR 检测通过将检测范围扩展到传统三重目标之外,包括 OROV 和 WNV,从而增强了流行病学 监测。这种操作优化缩短了周转时间,并防止对新兴虫媒病毒的诊断不足,成为流行地区的强大诊断工具。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
具有相似临床表现的多种虫媒病毒的共同循环对公共卫生提出了重大挑战 监测。
设计与对象
在巴西萨尔瓦多进行了一项诊断准确性研究,评估了 254 份前瞻性血清样本和 34 份存档的阳性样本。
核心方法
该检测的引物和探针是在计算机上设计的,针对登革热 (DENV)、寨卡 (ZIKV)、基孔肯雅 (CHIKV)、奥罗普切 (OROV) 和西尼罗河 (WNV) 病毒的高度保守区域。该方案在单个热循环的三个反应中进行了优化,包括一个四重反应(DENV、CHIKV、ZIKV 和 hRNAseP,内源性对照)和两个单重反应(OROV 和 WNV)。
主要结果
在前瞻性分析中,该检测确定了 45 例 DENV、3 例 CHIKV 和 9 例 OROV 阳性病例。DENV 和 CHIKV 的诊断准确率为 96%(95% CI:93.3-98.0%),成功检出参考标准漏检的 4 例 DENV 病例。结合 OROV 可以识别传统三重协议可能忽略的九种情况。
解释与创新
这种单循环多重 RT-qPCR 检测将检测范围扩展到传统三重靶标之外,包括 OROV 和 WNV,从而增强了流行病学 监测。这种操作优化缩短了周转时间,并防止对新兴虫媒病毒的诊断不足,成为流行地区的强大诊断工具。
科研与公卫意义
该测定改进了 CHIKV 和其他虫媒病毒的检测,有助于 监测 和流行地区虫媒病毒疾病的管理。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在特定地点进行的,并且使用的样本量有限,这可能会限制普遍性。CHIKV的诊断准确性较高,但样本量较小,可能影响研究结果的稳健性。

Development of a Single Thermal Cycling Multiplex RT-qPCR Assay for Rapid Arbovirus Detection

Authors: Róger J. Costa, Andreas Stöcker, Cleyde Sheyla Chachaqui Marconi, Gúbio Soares Campos, Sílvia Inês Sardi, Abevailton Miranda, Carlos Roberto Brites, Wei-Kung Wang, Eduardo Martins Netto
Original abstract (English)
Background: The co-circulation of multiple arboviruses with similar clinical presentations poses a significant challenge for public health surveillance. This study aimed to develop and clinically validate a novel multiplex RT-qPCR assay for the simultaneous detection of five arboviruses in three reactions. Methods: A diagnostic accuracy study was conducted in Salvador, Brazil. Evaluation was performed using 254 prospective serum samples and 34 archived positive samples. The primers and probes for the assay were designed in silico, all targeting highly conserved regions of Dengue (DENV), Zika (ZIKV), Chikungunya (CHIKV), Oropouche (OROV), and West Nile (WNV) viruses. The protocol was optimized in three reactions with a single thermal cycle, comprising a tetraplex reaction (DENV, CHIKV, ZIKV, and hRNAseP, an endogenous control) and two singleplex reactions (OROV and WNV). Results: In prospective analysis, the assay identified 45 DENV, 3 CHIKV, and 9 OROV-positive cases. Diagnostic accuracy for DENV and CHIKV was 96% (95% CI: 93.3–98.0%), successfully detecting four DENV cases missed by the reference standard. Furthermore, incorporating OROV enabled the identification of nine cases that conventional triplex protocols would have overlooked. Conclusion: This single-cycling multiplex RT-qPCR assay enhances epidemiological surveillance by expanding detection beyond traditional triplex targets to include OROV and WNV. This operational optimization reduces turnaround times and prevents the underdiagnosis of emerging arboviruses, serving as a robust diagnostic tool for endemic regions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The co-circulation of multiple arboviruses with similar clinical presentations poses a significant challenge for public health surveillance.
Design and population
A diagnostic accuracy study was conducted in Salvador, Brazil, evaluating 254 prospective serum samples and 34 archived positive samples.
Core methods
The primers and probes for the assay were designed in silico, targeting highly conserved regions of Dengue (DENV), Zika (ZIKV), Chikungunya (CHIKV), Oropouche (OROV), and West Nile (WNV) viruses. The protocol was optimized in three reactions with a single thermal cycle, comprising a tetraplex reaction (DENV, CHIKV, ZIKV, and hRNAseP, an endogenous control) and two singleplex reactions (OROV and WNV).
Main results
In prospective analysis, the assay identified 45 DENV, 3 CHIKV, and 9 OROV-positive cases. Diagnostic accuracy for DENV and CHIKV was 96% (95% CI: 93.3–98.0%), successfully detecting four DENV cases missed by the reference standard. Incorporating OROV enabled the identification of nine cases that conventional triplex protocols would have overlooked.
Interpretation and novelty
This single-cycling multiplex RT-qPCR assay enhances epidemiological surveillance by expanding detection beyond traditional triplex targets to include OROV and WNV. This operational optimization reduces turnaround times and prevents the underdiagnosis of emerging arboviruses, serving as a robust diagnostic tool for endemic regions.
Scientific and public-health significance
This assay improves the detection of CHIKV and other arboviruses, aiding in the surveillance and management of arboviral diseases in endemic regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a specific location and used a limited sample size, which may limit generalizability. The diagnostic accuracy for CHIKV was high, but the sample size was small, which may affect the robustness of the findings.

摘要中文翻译原始的宏观气候和流行病学时间序列本质上容易出现非平稳偏差和线性季节性趋势,导致虚假相关性掩盖了真正的环境遥相关性。本研究旨在评估和测量2012年1月至2023年12月(n = 144个月)全球海洋尼诺指数(ONI)与印度尼西亚当地基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)传播动态之间的长距离关系。在方法论上,RStudio 中采用了定量关联时间序列设计。两个数据集都表现出非正态分布 (p=0.000) 和通过增强迪基-富勒测试的原始非平稳性 (p>0.05)。为了消除数学偏差,应用一阶差分变换,成功实现了平稳性 (p=0.0100)。然后计算从滞后 -6 个月到滞后 +6 个月的交互窗口内的互相关函数 (CCF)。结果揭示了高度动态的双峰流行病学模式,峰值出现在 2013 年(15,324 例)和 2023 年(6,049 例)。虽然在 Lag 0 (r=-0.0180) 时没有发生瞬时相关性,但在 Lag -6 (r=0.1246) 时出现了显着的最佳正互相关。这证实了一种非对称遥相关,即 ONI 的增加(转向厄尔尼诺阶段)比当前时期提前六个月直接驱动印度尼西亚基孔肯雅热 暴发 的增加,为基于气候的早期预警系统提供了重要的基线。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估和测量2012年1月至2023年12月(n = 144个月)全球海洋尼诺指数(ONI)与印度尼西亚当地基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)传播动态之间的长距离关系。所提供的证据并未报告具体的科学/公共卫生问题或知识差距。
设计与对象
本研究采用 RStudio 内的定量关联时间序列设计来评估 2012 年 1 月至 2023 年 12 月(n = 144 个月)全球海洋厄尔尼诺指数 (ONI) 与印度尼西亚当地 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 传播 动态之间的关系。
核心方法
在方法论上,RStudio 中采用了定量关联时间序列设计。数据集表现出非正态分布和原始非平稳性,这些问题可以通过一阶差分变换来解决以实现平稳性。然后计算从滞后 -6 个月到滞后 +6 个月的交互窗口内的互相关函数 (CCF)。
主要结果
结果揭示了高度动态的双峰流行病学模式,峰值出现在 2013 年(15,324 例)和 2023 年(6,049 例)。在滞后 -6 (r=0.1246) 时出现了显着的最佳正互相关,证实了不对称遥相关,其中本期之前的 ONI 增加六个月直接驱动印度尼西亚基孔肯雅病 暴发 的增加。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证实了一种不对称的遥相关,即本期之前六个月的 ONI 增加直接导致印度尼西亚基孔肯雅热 暴发 的增加,为基于气候的早期预警系统提供了重要的基线。这一新发现表明气候指数与基孔肯雅热 传播 之间存在预测关系。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为印度尼西亚开发基于气候的基孔肯雅热 暴发 早期预警系统提供信息,从而加强 监测 和公共卫生准备。结果对风险评估和预防策略具有影响。
局限与证据强度
研究设计的局限性包括使用非平稳时间序列数据,这可能会引入偏差。样本量对于研究期间来说是足够的,但对其他地区或时间段的普遍性尚不确定。由于时间序列分析的局限性和需要进一步验证,证据强度中等。

Index

Authors: Yonatan Yolius Anggara, Ery Surya Sevriana
Original abstract (English)
Raw macro-climatic and epidemiological time series are inherently prone to non-stationary biases and linear seasonal trends, causing spurious correlations that mask genuine environmental teleconnections. This study aimed to evaluate and measure the long-distance relationship between the global Oceanic Niño Index (ONI) and local Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) transmission dynamics in Indonesia from January 2012 to December 2023 (n = 144 months). Methodologically, a quantitative associative time-series design was employed within RStudio. Both datasets exhibited non-normal distributions (p=0.000) and raw non-stationarity via Augmented Dickey-Fuller testing (p>0.05). To eliminate mathematical bias, a first-order differencing transformation was applied, successfully achieving stationarity (p=0.0100). The Cross-Correlation Function (CCF) was then calculated across an interaction window from Lag -6 to Lag +6 months. The results revealed a highly dynamic double-peak epidemiological pattern, peaking in 2013 (15,324 cases) and 2023 (6,049 cases). While no instantaneous correlation occurred at Lag 0 (r=-0.0180), a significant, optimal positive cross-correlation emerged at Lag -6 (r=0.1246). This confirms an asymmetric teleconnection where an increase in the ONI—shifting toward an El Niño phase—preceding the current period by six months directly drives increased Chikungunya outbreaks in Indonesia, providing a vital baseline for climate-based Early Warning Systems.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate and measure the long-distance relationship between the global Oceanic Niño Index (ONI) and local Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) transmission dynamics in Indonesia from January 2012 to December 2023 (n = 144 months). The supplied evidence does not report a specific scientific/public-health problem or knowledge gap.
Design and population
This study employed a quantitative associative time-series design within RStudio to evaluate the relationship between the global Oceanic Niño Index (ONI) and local Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) transmission dynamics in Indonesia from January 2012 to December 2023 (n = 144 months).
Core methods
Methodologically, a quantitative associative time-series design was employed within RStudio. The datasets exhibited non-normal distributions and raw non-stationarity, which were addressed through a first-order differencing transformation to achieve stationarity. The Cross-Correlation Function (CCF) was then calculated across an interaction window from Lag -6 to Lag +6 months.
Main results
The results revealed a highly dynamic double-peak epidemiological pattern, peaking in 2013 (15,324 cases) and 2023 (6,049 cases). A significant, optimal positive cross-correlation emerged at Lag -6 (r=0.1246), confirming an asymmetric teleconnection where an increase in the ONI preceding the current period by six months directly drives increased Chikungunya outbreaks in Indonesia.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as confirming an asymmetric teleconnection where an increase in the ONI preceding the current period by six months directly drives increased Chikungunya outbreaks in Indonesia, providing a vital baseline for climate-based Early Warning Systems. This novel finding suggests a predictive relationship between climate indices and Chikungunya transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of climate-based Early Warning Systems for Chikungunya outbreaks in Indonesia, enhancing surveillance and public health preparedness. The results have implications for risk assessment and prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study design limitations include the use of non-stationary time series data, which may introduce biases. The sample size is adequate for the study period, but the generalisability to other regions or time periods is uncertain. The evidence strength is moderate due to the limitations in the time series analysis and the need for further validation.

通过流式细胞术分析虫媒病毒感染组织的细胞群。

Analysis of Cell Populations from Arbovirus-Infected Tissues by Flow Cytometry.
作者： Martins JR, Batista VL, Queiroz-Junior CM, Teixeira MM, Costa VV
摘要中文翻译虫媒病毒仍然是世界许多地区公共卫生的重大挑战。尽管具有共同的流行病学特征,但每种虫媒病毒性疾病都表现出不同的细胞向性和激活模式,并发展为尚未完全阐明的免疫病理学结果。要更深入地了解这些过程,需要能够表征通常复杂的细胞反应的方法。在这种背景下,流式细胞术成为一种有价值的工具,可以表征异质细胞群、评估活化和增殖标记、量化炎症介质以及检测细胞死亡途径。通过整合这些参数,流式细胞术可以增强我们对虫媒病毒发病机制的了解,阐明其细胞向性,并了解免疫应答的感染情况。这篇方法论文章概述了将该技术应用于受基孔肯雅热、登革热、黄热病和寨卡病毒感染的不同目标组织的主要步骤,强调了最有效的策略。

局限与证据强度

Analysis of Cell Populations from Arbovirus-Infected Tissues by Flow Cytometry.

Authors: Martins JR, Batista VL, Queiroz-Junior CM, Teixeira MM, Costa VV
Original abstract (English)
Arboviruses still represent a major challenge to public health in many parts of the world. Despite shared epidemiological characteristics, each arboviral disorder displays distinct cellular tropism and activation patterns, progressing to immunopathological outcomes that remain not fully elucidated. A deeper understanding of these processes requires methodologies capable of characterizing the often-complex cellular responses. In this context, flow cytometry emerges as a valuable tool, enabling the characterization of heterogeneous cell populations, the assessment of activation and proliferation markers, the quantification of inflammatory mediators, and the detection of cell death pathways. By integrating these parameters, flow cytometry can enhance our understanding of arbovirus pathogenesis, clarify their cellular tropism, and understand immune responses to infection. This methodological article outlines the main steps for applying the technique to different target tissues infected by chikungunya, dengue, yellow fever, and Zika viruses, emphasizing the most effective strategies.

巴西虫媒病毒传播:范围审查协议

Arbovirus circulation in Brazil: a scoping review protocol
作者： Romário de Sousa Oliveira, Valéria Cristina Soares Pinheiro
摘要中文翻译由伊蚊物种(主要是埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊)传播的虫媒病毒是巴西的一项重大公共卫生挑战。近几十年来,登革热病毒 (DENV)、寨卡病毒 (ZIKV)、基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 的共同传播、重新出现和反复流行,以及黄热病病毒 (YFV) 潜在的再城市化风险,给不同地理区域带来了严重的健康、社会和经济负担。尽管进行了广泛的流行病学 监测 和研究,但关于感染频率、人群易感性、诊断方法和区域疾病负担的局部差异的文献仍然支离破碎。对已发表的证据进行系统绘制——特别关注实验室检测方法(分子、血清学或病毒分离)证明的人类感染和暴露——对于综合当前知识、描绘时空模式并确定巴西城市和城郊环境中伊蚊传播虫媒病毒研究的关键证据差距是必要的。

Arbovirus circulation in Brazil: a scoping review protocol

Authors: Romário de Sousa Oliveira, Valéria Cristina Soares Pinheiro
Original abstract (English)
Arboviruses transmitted by Aedes species—predominantly Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus—represent a major public health challenge in Brazil. The co-circulation, re-emergence, and recurring epidemics of Dengue virus (DENV), Zika virus (ZIKV), Chikungunya virus (CHIKV), alongside the potential re-urbanization risk of Yellow Fever virus (YFV) over recent decades, have imposed severe health, social, and economic burdens across diverse geographic regions. Despite extensive epidemiological surveillance and research, literature remains fragmented regarding local variations in infection frequency, population susceptibility, diagnostic approaches, and regional disease burdens. A systematic mapping of published evidence—specifically focusing on human infection and exposure evidenced by laboratory detection methods (molecular, serological, or viral isolation)—is necessary to synthesize current knowledge, delineate spatial-temporal patterns, and identify critical evidence gaps in Aedes-borne arboviral research across Brazilian urban and peri-urban environments.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

木蓝作为抗虫媒病毒植物成分的来源:生物引导分馏、3-O-β-D-葡萄糖基-β-谷甾醇的分离,以及针对 ZIKV、CHIKV 和 MAYV 的体外评估

Indigofera suffruticosa as a Source of Anti-Arboviral Phytoconstituents: Bioguided Fractionation, Isolation of 3-O-β-D-Glucosyl-β-Sitosterol, and In Vitro Evaluation Against ZIKV, CHIKV, and MAYV
作者： Luana Beatriz Araújo Vaz, Maria Luísa Alves Majevski, Adriana Cotta Cardoso Reis, Viviane Renata Scalon, Cíntia Lopes de Brito Magalhães, Breno de Mello Silva, Geraldo Célio Brandão
摘要中文翻译由伊蚊传播的虫媒病毒,包括寨卡病毒 (ZIKV)、基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 和马亚罗病毒 (MAYV),缺乏已获批准的 抗病毒药物 疗法。本研究评估了靛蓝茎叶乙醇提取物和级分的细胞毒性和 抗病毒药物 活性。(豆科),并通过生物引导分馏分离出活性成分。通过冷浸渍获得提取物并分配成氯仿、乙酸乙酯和甲醇水溶液级分。通过 MRC-5 和 Vero 细胞中的 MTT 测定评估细胞毒性,并在 Vero 细胞中同时进行 治疗 感染测定来测定 抗病毒药物 活性。茎乙醇提取物显示出有效的抗 ZIKV 活性(EC50 = 29.43 ± 1.43 μg/mL;SI > 13.59)。氯仿馏分显示出广谱功效,EC50 = 89.48 ± 2.70 μg/mL (ZIKV;SI > 4.47)、37.05 ± 2.26 μg/mL (CHIKV;SI > 10.80) 和 42.39 ± 3.60 μg/mL (MAYV;SI > 9.44)。生物引导分级分离得到3-O-β-D-葡萄糖基-β-谷甾醇,其对CHIKV(EC50 = 151.76 μM;SI = 2.02)和MAYV(EC50 = 150.35 μM;SI = 2.03)表现出选择性抗甲病毒活性,但对ZIKV没有活性,表明其他成分导致了抗黄病毒作用。靛蓝是广谱抗虫媒病毒药物的有前景的来源,支持进一步的生物测定引导的分离和机制研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由伊蚊传播的虫媒病毒,包括寨卡病毒 (ZIKV)、基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 和马亚罗病毒 (MAYV),缺乏已获批准的 抗病毒药物 疗法。本研究旨在鉴定靛蓝中潜在的抗虫媒病毒化合物。
设计与对象
本研究评估了靛蓝茎叶乙醇提取物和级分的细胞毒性和 抗病毒药物 活性。该研究是使用 Vero 细胞作为 宿主 在体外进行的。
核心方法
通过 MRC-5 和 Vero 细胞中的 MTT 测定评估细胞毒性,并在 Vero 细胞中同时进行 治疗 感染测定来测定 抗病毒药物 活性。通过冷浸渍获得提取物并分配成氯仿、乙酸乙酯和甲醇水溶液级分。使用生物引导分级分离来分离活性成分。
主要结果
茎乙醇提取物显示出有效的抗 ZIKV 活性(EC50 = 29.43 ± 1.43 μg/mL;SI > 13.59)。氯仿馏分显示出广谱功效,EC50 = 89.48 ± 2.70 μg/mL (ZIKV;SI > 4.47)、37.05 ± 2.26 μg/mL (CHIKV;SI > 10.80) 和 42.39 ± 3.60 μg/mL (MAYV;SI > 9.44)。
解释与创新
该研究确定 3-O-β-D-葡萄糖基-β-谷甾醇是一种对 CHIKV 和 MAYV 具有选择性抗甲病毒活性的化合物,但对 ZIKV 没有,这表明其他成分可能是抗黄病毒作用的原因。
科研与公卫意义
木蓝是广谱抗虫媒病毒药物的有前景的来源,支持进一步生物测定引导的分离和机制研究以实现潜在的治疗应用。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外评估,研究结果并未考虑体内疗效或临床相关性。证据是温和的,因为它是基于没有人体或动物试验数据的实验室测定。

Indigofera suffruticosa as a Source of Anti-Arboviral Phytoconstituents: Bioguided Fractionation, Isolation of 3-O-β-D-Glucosyl-β-Sitosterol, and In Vitro Evaluation Against ZIKV, CHIKV, and MAYV

Authors: Luana Beatriz Araújo Vaz, Maria Luísa Alves Majevski, Adriana Cotta Cardoso Reis, Viviane Renata Scalon, Cíntia Lopes de Brito Magalhães, Breno de Mello Silva, Geraldo Célio Brandão
Original abstract (English)
Arboviruses transmitted by Aedes mosquitoes, including Zika (ZIKV), Chikungunya (CHIKV), and Mayaro (MAYV) viruses, lack approved antiviral therapies. This study evaluated the cytotoxicity and antiviral activity of ethanolic extracts and fractions from stems and leaves of Indigofera suffruticosa Mill. (Fabaceae), and isolated the active principles through bioguided fractionation. Extracts were obtained by cold maceration and partitioned into chloroform, ethyl acetate, and aqueous methanol fractions. Cytotoxicity was assessed by the MTT assay in MRC-5 and Vero cells, and antiviral activity was determined in simultaneous treatment-infection assays in Vero cells. The stem ethanolic extract showed potent anti-ZIKV activity (EC50 = 29.43 ± 1.43 μg/mL; SI > 13.59). The chloroform fraction displayed broad-spectrum efficacy, with EC50 = 89.48 ± 2.70 μg/mL (ZIKV; SI > 4.47), 37.05 ± 2.26 μg/mL (CHIKV; SI > 10.80), and 42.39 ± 3.60 μg/mL (MAYV; SI > 9.44). Bioguided fractionation afforded 3-O-β-D-glucosyl-β-sitosterol, which exhibited selective anti-alphavirus activity against CHIKV (EC50 = 151.76 μM; SI = 2.02) and MAYV (EC50 = 150.35 μM; SI = 2.03), but was inactive against ZIKV, indicating that additional constituents account for the anti-flaviviral effect. Indigofera suffruticosa is a promising source of broad-spectrum anti-arboviral agents, supporting further bioassay-guided isolation and mechanistic studies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Arboviruses transmitted by Aedes mosquitoes, including Zika (ZIKV), Chikungunya (CHIKV), and Mayaro (MAYV) viruses, lack approved antiviral therapies. This study aimed to identify potential anti-arboviral compounds from Indigofera suffruticosa Mill.
Design and population
This study evaluated the cytotoxicity and antiviral activity of ethanolic extracts and fractions from stems and leaves of Indigofera suffruticosa Mill. The study was conducted in vitro using Vero cells as the host.
Core methods
Cytotoxicity was assessed by the MTT assay in MRC-5 and Vero cells, and antiviral activity was determined in simultaneous treatment-infection assays in Vero cells. Extracts were obtained by cold maceration and partitioned into chloroform, ethyl acetate, and aqueous methanol fractions. Bioguided fractionation was used to isolate the active principles.
Main results
The stem ethanolic extract showed potent anti-ZIKV activity (EC50 = 29.43 ± 1.43 μg/mL; SI > 13.59). The chloroform fraction displayed broad-spectrum efficacy, with EC50 = 89.48 ± 2.70 μg/mL (ZIKV; SI > 4.47), 37.05 ± 2.26 μg/mL (CHIKV; SI > 10.80), and 42.39 ± 3.60 μg/mL (MAYV; SI > 9.44).
Interpretation and novelty
The study identified 3-O-β-D-glucosyl-β-sitosterol as a compound with selective anti-alphavirus activity against CHIKV and MAYV, but not ZIKV, suggesting that additional constituents may be responsible for the anti-flaviviral effect.
Scientific and public-health significance
Indigofera suffruticosa is a promising source of broad-spectrum anti-arboviral agents, supporting further bioassay-guided isolation and mechanistic studies for potential therapeutic applications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to in vitro evaluations, and the findings do not account for in vivo efficacy or clinical relevance. The evidence is moderate as it is based on laboratory assays without human or animal trial data.

基于微乳液的薄荷精油输送作为控制埃及伊蚊的可持续策略

Microemulsion-based delivery of peppermint essential oil as a sustainable strategy for Aedes aegypti control
作者： Carolina Paula de Souza Moreira, Natasha Reynaud, Marcos Paulo Gomes Mol, Gilson Faria, Sérgio Caldas
摘要中文翻译背景 登革热、寨卡病毒和基孔肯雅热等虫媒病毒性疾病仍然是全球主要的公共卫生挑战。在缺乏广泛有效的 抗病毒药物 疗法的情况下,并考虑到当前 疫苗接种 策略的局限性,媒介 控制仍然是减少疾病 传播 的主要方法。精油因其可再生来源、生物降解性和多种作用机制而成为合成杀虫剂的有前景的替代品。然而,它们的实际应用往往受到水溶性差和物理化学不稳定性的限制。本研究研究了胡椒薄荷精油的化学成分、杀幼虫活性和微乳液制剂对埃及伊蚊幼虫的作用。方法 采用气相色谱-质谱法(GC-MS)测定精油的化学成分。通过测定死亡率率和中位致死浓度(LC 50 )来评估针对三龄埃及伊蚊幼虫的杀幼虫活性。构建了伪三元相图来确定合适的微乳液区域,并根据液滴尺寸、多分散指数 (PDI) 和短期物理稳定性对所选配方进行表征。随后评估了不同精油浓度下所得微乳液的杀幼虫活性。还评估了含有不含胡椒胡椒精油的制剂组分的空白微乳液作为对照。结果 GC-MS 分析鉴定出 14 种化合物,几乎代表了精油成分的 100%,其中薄荷醇 (50.17%) 和薄荷酮 (23.02%) 是主要成分。游离精油对埃及伊蚊表现出浓度依赖性杀幼虫活性,LC 50 为 175.7 μg/mL,完整幼虫 死亡率 为 250 μg/mL。在测试的配方中,PME-05被选为最有希望进一步开发的配方,因为它在仅含有5%薄荷精油的情况下实现了完整的幼虫死亡率,并且表现出最低的PDI(0.06±0.007),表明高度均匀的分散体系。结论 结果支持使用微乳液技术作为胡椒胡椒精油的合适输送系统,促进其在水性环境中的分散和应用,同时保留其杀幼虫活性。所开发的制剂为植物杀幼虫剂在蚊子控制中的应用提供了一个有前途的平台。需要进一步的研究来评估长期理化稳定性、残留活性、对非目标生物的影响以及半田间和田间条件下的性能。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热、寨卡病毒和基孔肯雅热等虫媒病毒性疾病仍然是全球重大的公共卫生挑战。在缺乏广泛有效的 抗病毒药物 疗法的情况下,并考虑到当前 疫苗接种 策略的局限性,媒介 控制仍然是减少疾病 传播 的主要方法。
设计与对象
本研究研究了胡椒薄荷精油的化学成分、杀幼虫活性和微乳液制剂对埃及伊蚊幼虫的作用。游离精油对埃及伊蚊表现出浓度依赖性杀幼虫活性,LC 50 为 175.7 μg/mL,完整幼虫 死亡率 为 250 μg/mL。
核心方法
通过气相色谱-质谱法(GC-MS)测定精油的化学成分。构建了伪三元相图来确定合适的微乳液区域,并根据液滴尺寸、多分散指数 (PDI) 和短期物理稳定性对所选配方进行表征。
主要结果
GC-MS 分析鉴定出 14 种化合物,几乎代表了精油成分的 100%,其中薄荷醇 (50.17%) 和薄荷酮 (23.02%) 是主要成分。在测试的配方中,PME-05被选为最有希望进一步开发的配方,因为它在仅含有5%薄荷精油的情况下实现了完整的幼虫死亡率,并且表现出最低的PDI(0.06±0.007),表明高度均匀的分散体系。
解释与创新
结果支持使用微乳液技术作为薄荷精油的合适输送系统,促进其在水性环境中的分散和应用,同时保留其杀幼虫活性。所开发的制剂为植物杀幼虫剂在蚊子控制中的应用提供了一个有前途的平台。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,基于微乳液的递送系统可以提供一种可持续且有效的方法来控制埃及伊蚊,埃及伊蚊是基孔肯雅热和其他虫媒病毒的 媒介。这可以为未来环保型 媒介 控制策略的研究和开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
需要进一步的研究来评估长期理化稳定性、残留活性、对非目标生物的影响以及半田间和田间条件下的性能。证据是温和的,因为它基于实验室发现,不包括现场验证。

Microemulsion-based delivery of peppermint essential oil as a sustainable strategy for Aedes aegypti control

Authors: Carolina Paula de Souza Moreira, Natasha Reynaud, Marcos Paulo Gomes Mol, Gilson Faria, Sérgio Caldas
Original abstract (English)
Background Arboviral diseases such as dengue, Zika, and chikungunya remain major public health challenges worldwide. In the absence of broadly effective antiviral therapies and considering the limitations of current vaccination strategies, vector control remains the primary approach for reducing disease transmission. Essential oils have emerged as promising alternatives to synthetic insecticides because of their renewable origin, biodegradability, and multiple mechanisms of action. Nevertheless, their practical application is often limited by poor water solubility and physicochemical instability. This study investigated the chemical composition, larvicidal activity, and microemulsion-based formulation of Mentha piperita L. essential oil against Aedes aegypti larvae. Methods The chemical composition of the essential oil was determined by gas chromatography–mass spectrometry (GC–MS). Larvicidal activity against third-instar A. aegypti larvae was evaluated by determining mortality rates and the median lethal concentration (LC 50 ). A pseudoternary phase diagram was constructed to identify suitable microemulsion regions, and selected formulations were characterized according to droplet size, polydispersity index (PDI), and short-term physical stability. The larvicidal activity of the resulting microemulsions was subsequently evaluated at different essential-oil concentrations. A blank microemulsion containing the formulation components without M. piperita essential oil was also evaluated as a control. Results GC–MS analysis identified 14 compounds representing nearly 100% of the essential oil composition, with menthol (50.17%) and menthone (23.02%) as the predominant constituents. The free essential oil exhibited concentration-dependent larvicidal activity against A. aegypti , with an LC 50 of 175.7 μg/mL and complete larval mortality at 250 μg/mL. Among the tested formulations, PME-05 was selected as the most promising formulation for further development because it achieved complete larval mortality while containing only 5% peppermint essential oil and exhibited the lowest PDI (0.06 ± 0.007), indicating a highly homogeneous dispersed system. Conclusion The results support the use of microemulsion technology as a suitable delivery system for M. piperita essential oil, facilitating its dispersion and application in aqueous environments while retaining its larvicidal activity. The developed formulation represents a promising platform for the application of botanical larvicides in mosquito control. Further studies are needed to evaluate long-term physicochemical stability, residual activity, effects on non-target organisms, and performance under semi-field and field conditions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Arboviral diseases such as dengue, Zika, and chikungunya remain major public health challenges worldwide. In the absence of broadly effective antiviral therapies and considering the limitations of current vaccination strategies, vector control remains the primary approach for reducing disease transmission.
Design and population
This study investigated the chemical composition, larvicidal activity, and microemulsion-based formulation of Mentha piperita L. essential oil against Aedes aegypti larvae. The free essential oil exhibited concentration-dependent larvicidal activity against Aedes aegypti, with an LC 50 of 175.7 μg/mL and complete larval mortality at 250 μg/mL.
Core methods
The chemical composition of the essential oil was determined by gas chromatography–mass spectrometry (GC–MS). A pseudoternary phase diagram was constructed to identify suitable microemulsion regions, and selected formulations were characterized according to droplet size, polydispersity index (PDI), and short-term physical stability.
Main results
GC–MS analysis identified 14 compounds representing nearly 100% of the essential oil composition, with menthol (50.17%) and menthone (23.02%) as the predominant constituents. Among the tested formulations, PME-05 was selected as the most promising formulation for further development because it achieved complete larval mortality while containing only 5% peppermint essential oil and exhibited the lowest PDI (0.06 ± 0.007), indicating a highly homogeneous dispersed system.
Interpretation and novelty
The results support the use of microemulsion technology as a suitable delivery system for Mentha piperita L. essential oil, facilitating its dispersion and application in aqueous environments while retaining its larvicidal activity. The developed formulation represents a promising platform for the application of botanical larvicides in mosquito control.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that microemulsion-based delivery systems could offer a sustainable and effective method for controlling Aedes aegypti, which is a vector for chikungunya and other arboviruses. This could inform future research and development of eco-friendly vector control strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
Further studies are needed to evaluate long-term physicochemical stability, residual activity, effects on non-target organisms, and performance under semi-field and field conditions. The evidence is moderate as it is based on laboratory findings and does not include field validation.
